<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#120d17">
  <meta name="description" content="Interactive Sacred Inferno Elite Four rerun decision guide for PokeMMO.">
  <title>Sacred Inferno — E4 Rerun Guide</title>
  <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 64 64'%3E%3Crect width='64' height='64' rx='14' fill='%23120d17'/%3E%3Cpath d='M34 8c3 11-7 14-2 23 2-7 8-8 10-15 9 9 14 18 10 29-3 9-11 14-21 14S12 53 11 43c-1-10 6-18 14-25 0 7 2 10 5 13-2-10 6-15 4-23Z' fill='%23ff6b35'/%3E%3Cpath d='M31 34c1 6-5 8-3 13 1-3 4-4 5-8 5 5 6 12 1 16-6 4-13 0-13-7 0-5 4-10 8-14 0 4 1 5 2 7 0-3 1-5 0-7Z' fill='%23ffd166'/%3E%3C/svg%3E">
  <style>
    :root {
      color-scheme: dark;
      --bg: #0e0a12;
      --panel: #18121f;
      --panel-2: #21172a;
      --panel-3: #2a1d33;
      --text: #fbf7ff;
      --muted: #b9acbf;
      --line: rgba(255,255,255,.10);
      --ember: #ff6b35;
      --gold: #ffd166;
      --violet: #a78bfa;
      --green: #66d9a3;
      --shadow: 0 18px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.32);
      --radius: 18px;
    }

    * { box-sizing: border-box; }
    html { scroll-behavior: smooth; }
    body {
      margin: 0;
      min-height: 100vh;
      background:
        radial-gradient(circle at 84% -10%, rgba(255,107,53,.16), transparent 32rem),
        radial-gradient(circle at -10% 35%, rgba(167,139,250,.10), transparent 30rem),
        var(--bg);
      color: var(--text);
      font-family: Inter, ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
      font-size: 16px;
      line-height: 1.5;
    }

    button, input { font: inherit; }
    button { color: inherit; }
    button:focus-visible, input:focus-visible {
      outline: 3px solid rgba(255,209,102,.75);
      outline-offset: 3px;
    }

    .shell { width: min(1120px, calc(100% - 32px)); margin: 0 auto; padding: 28px 0 72px; }
    .topbar { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 18px; margin-bottom: 28px; }
    .brand { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; min-width: 0; }
    .mark {
      width: 44px; height: 44px; flex: 0 0 auto; display: grid; place-items: center;
      border-radius: 14px; background: linear-gradient(145deg, #ff8b43, #e83f27);
      box-shadow: 0 10px 26px rgba(255,89,46,.24); font-size: 1.35rem;
    }
    .brand h1 { font-size: clamp(1.05rem, 3vw, 1.35rem); margin: 0; letter-spacing: -.025em; }
    .brand p { margin: 1px 0 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: .8rem; letter-spacing: .08em; text-transform: uppercase; }
    .ghost-button {
      border: 1px solid var(--line); background: rgba(255,255,255,.035); border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 9px 14px; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap; transition: .18s ease;
    }
    .ghost-button:hover { border-color: rgba(255,255,255,.24); background: rgba(255,255,255,.07); }

    .hero { margin-bottom: 20px; }
    .eyebrow { color: var(--ember); font-size: .78rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: .13em; text-transform: uppercase; }
    .hero h2 { margin: 7px 0 8px; font-size: clamp(2rem, 7vw, 4.3rem); line-height: .98; letter-spacing: -.06em; max-width: 850px; }
    .hero-copy { max-width: 720px; color: var(--muted); margin: 0; font-size: 1.03rem; }
    .warning {
      display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: flex-start; margin: 22px 0 26px; padding: 14px 16px;
      color: #ffe7a7; background: rgba(255,196,61,.08); border: 1px solid rgba(255,196,61,.24); border-radius: 14px;
    }
    .warning strong { color: var(--gold); }

    .progress { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin: 0 0 16px; min-height: 34px; }
    .crumb { color: var(--muted); font-size: .86rem; }
    .crumb.current { color: var(--text); font-weight: 750; }
    .crumb-sep { color: #6d6174; }

    .panel {
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(34,25,42,.96), rgba(24,18,31,.98));
      border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--radius); box-shadow: var(--shadow); overflow: hidden;
    }
    .panel-head { display: flex; align-items: flex-end; justify-content: space-between; gap: 16px; padding: 22px 22px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
    .panel-head h3 { margin: 2px 0 0; font-size: clamp(1.35rem, 4vw, 2rem); letter-spacing: -.035em; }
    .panel-head p { margin: 3px 0 0; color: var(--muted); }
    .step-label { color: var(--violet); font-size: .75rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: .12em; text-transform: uppercase; }
    .panel-body { padding: 22px; }

    .region-tabs { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(5, 1fr); gap: 9px; }
    .tab {
      min-height: 52px; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 13px; background: rgba(255,255,255,.025);
      cursor: pointer; font-weight: 750; transition: transform .15s ease, border-color .15s ease, background .15s ease;
    }
    .tab:hover { transform: translateY(-1px); border-color: rgba(255,107,53,.42); }
    .tab.active { background: linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(255,107,53,.24), rgba(255,107,53,.10)); border-color: rgba(255,107,53,.68); color: #fff3ed; }

    .trainer-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(176px, 1fr)); gap: 12px; }
    .trainer-card {
      position: relative; min-height: 116px; padding: 18px; text-align: left; border-radius: 16px;
      border: 1px solid var(--line); background: rgba(255,255,255,.025); cursor: pointer; overflow: hidden;
      transition: transform .16s ease, border-color .16s ease, background .16s ease;
    }
    .trainer-card::after {
      content: ""; position: absolute; inset: auto -28px -46px auto; width: 100px; height: 100px;
      border-radius: 50%; background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(255,107,53,.25), transparent 65%); pointer-events: none;
    }
    .trainer-card:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); border-color: rgba(167,139,250,.48); background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); }
    .trainer-card span { display: block; }
    .trainer-name { font-size: 1.13rem; font-weight: 820; letter-spacing: -.02em; }
    .trainer-meta { color: var(--muted); font-size: .82rem; margin-top: 8px; }
    .trainer-number { color: var(--ember); font-size: .75rem; font-weight: 850; letter-spacing: .1em; margin-bottom: 20px; }

    .search-wrap { position: relative; margin-bottom: 16px; }
    .search-wrap svg { position: absolute; left: 15px; top: 50%; transform: translateY(-50%); color: var(--muted); pointer-events: none; }
    .search {
      width: 100%; min-height: 50px; padding: 12px 16px 12px 44px; color: var(--text); background: #120e18;
      border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 13px;
    }
    .search::placeholder { color: #827689; }
    .lead-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(145px, 1fr)); gap: 9px; }
    .lead-button, .choice-button {
      border: 1px solid var(--line); background: rgba(255,255,255,.035); border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 13px 14px; cursor: pointer; text-align: left; font-weight: 720; transition: .15s ease;
    }
    .lead-button:hover, .choice-button:hover { border-color: rgba(255,107,53,.65); background: rgba(255,107,53,.10); transform: translateY(-1px); }
    .lead-button mark { color: var(--gold); background: transparent; }
    .empty { color: var(--muted); text-align: center; padding: 26px 8px; }

    .walkthrough { display: grid; gap: 14px; }
    .path-card { border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 16px; background: rgba(255,255,255,.025); overflow: hidden; }
    .path-card.past { opacity: .74; }
    .path-card-head { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); color: var(--muted); font-size: .82rem; }
    .path-card-head strong { color: var(--text); }
    .turn-badge { display: grid; place-items: center; width: 27px; height: 27px; border-radius: 9px; background: rgba(167,139,250,.14); color: #d8ccff; font-weight: 850; }
    .actions { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 8px 16px 14px; }
    .action { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 28px 1fr; gap: 10px; align-items: start; padding: 9px 0; }
    .action + .action { border-top: 1px dashed rgba(255,255,255,.07); }
    .action-num { display: grid; place-items: center; width: 25px; height: 25px; border-radius: 50%; background: rgba(255,107,53,.15); color: #ff9b75; font-size: .75rem; font-weight: 850; }
    .action.setup .action-num { background: rgba(255,209,102,.13); color: var(--gold); }
    .action.setup .action-text { color: #ffe7a7; font-weight: 730; }
    .action-text { padding-top: 1px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
    .response { margin: 0 16px 14px; padding: 10px 12px; border-radius: 11px; background: rgba(167,139,250,.10); color: #d8ccff; font-size: .9rem; }

    .decision { padding: 18px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,107,53,.34); border-radius: 16px; background: rgba(255,107,53,.065); }
    .decision h4 { margin: 0 0 12px; font-size: 1.08rem; }
    .choices { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr)); gap: 9px; }
    .choice-button { background: rgba(255,107,53,.08); border-color: rgba(255,107,53,.28); }
    .choice-button::after { content: " →"; color: var(--ember); }
    .complete { padding: 19px; border: 1px solid rgba(102,217,163,.32); border-radius: 16px; background: rgba(102,217,163,.075); }
    .complete strong { display: block; color: var(--green); font-size: 1.06rem; margin-bottom: 3px; }
    .complete p { margin: 0; color: var(--muted); }
    .walk-controls { display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 2px; }
    .control { border: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--panel-3); border-radius: 12px; padding: 11px 15px; cursor: pointer; }
    .control.primary { border-color: rgba(255,107,53,.42); background: rgba(255,107,53,.13); }
    .control:disabled { opacity: .4; cursor: not-allowed; }

    .stats { color: var(--muted); font-size: .78rem; text-align: center; margin-top: 18px; }
    .hidden { display: none !important; }

    @media (max-width: 720px) {
      .shell { width: min(100% - 22px, 1120px); padding-top: 18px; }
      .topbar { align-items: flex-start; }
      .brand p { display: none; }
      .hero h2 { font-size: clamp(2.25rem, 14vw, 3.5rem); }
      .panel-head { align-items: flex-start; flex-direction: column; padding: 18px 17px 14px; }
      .panel-body { padding: 17px; }
      .region-tabs { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
      .region-tabs .tab:last-child { grid-column: 1 / -1; }
      .trainer-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
      .lead-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }
    }

    @media (max-width: 390px) {
      .trainer-grid, .lead-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
      .ghost-button { padding: 8px 11px; }
    }

    @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
      html { scroll-behavior: auto; }
      *, *::before, *::after { transition: none !important; }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main class="shell">
    <header class="topbar">
      <div class="brand">
        <div class="mark" aria-hidden="true">🔥</div>
        <div>
          <h1>Sacred Inferno</h1>
          <p>Elite Four rerun guide</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button class="ghost-button" id="resetTop" type="button">Start over</button>
    </header>

    <section class="hero" aria-labelledby="page-title">
      <div class="eyebrow">PokeMMO • Exact source routes</div>
      <h2 id="page-title">Choose what happened. Follow the solve.</h2>
      <p class="hero-copy">Select a region, opponent, and lead. After each action, tap the opponent’s response to continue along the correct branch.</p>
    </section>

    <aside class="warning" id="warningBox">
      <span aria-hidden="true">⚠️</span>
      <div><strong>Rerun requirement:</strong> <span id="warningText"></span></div>
    </aside>

    <nav class="progress" id="breadcrumbs" aria-label="Guide progress"></nav>

    <section class="panel" aria-live="polite">
      <div class="panel-head">
        <div>
          <div class="step-label" id="stepLabel">Step 1</div>
          <h3 id="panelTitle">Choose a region</h3>
          <p id="panelSubtitle">All five Elite Four rerun routes are included.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="panel-body">
        <div id="regionView"></div>
        <div id="trainerView" class="hidden"></div>
        <div id="leadView" class="hidden"></div>
        <div id="solveView" class="hidden"></div>
      </div>
    </section>
    <p class="stats" id="stats"></p>
  </main>

Rock"},{"id":"a1","kind":"action","text":"Teleport"},{"id":"a2","kind":"action","text":"switch to Slowbro"},{"id":"a3","kind":"action","text":"Yawn"},{"id":"a4","kind":"action","text":"Teleport"},{"id":"a5","kind":"action","text":"Volcarona Quiver Dance x1"}],"branch":null}}}}</script>
  <script>
    (() => {
      "use strict";
      const allData = JSON.parse(document.getElementById("guide-data").textContent);
      const regionOrder = ["kanto", "hoenn", "sinnoh", "johto", "unova"];
      const regionNames = { kanto: "Kanto", hoenn: "Hoenn", sinnoh: "Sinnoh", johto: "Johto", unova: "Unova" };
      const state = { region: null, groupIndex: null, lead: null, path: [], choices: [] };

      const el = id => document.getElementById(id);
      const views = ["regionView", "trainerView", "leadView", "solveView"];
      const showOnly = id => views.forEach(view => el(view).classList.toggle("hidden", view !== id));
      const make = (tag, className, text) => {
        const node = document.createElement(tag);
        if (className) node.className = className;
        if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
        return node;
      };
      const currentData = () => state.region ? allData[state.region] : null;
      const currentGroup = () => currentData()?.groups[state.groupIndex] ?? null;

      function setHeading(step, title, subtitle) {
        el("stepLabel").textContent = `Step ${step}`;
        el("panelTitle").textContent = title;
        el("panelSubtitle").textContent = subtitle;
      }

      function updateHash() {
        const parts = [];
        if (state.region) parts.push(state.region);
        if (state.groupIndex !== null) parts.push(String(state.groupIndex));
        if (state.lead) parts.push(encodeURIComponent(state.lead.label));
        if (state.choices.length) parts.push(state.choices.map(encodeURIComponent).join("~"));
        history.replaceState(null, "", parts.length ? `#${parts.join("/")}` : location.pathname + location.search);
      }

      function updateBreadcrumbs() {
        const nav = el("breadcrumbs");
        nav.replaceChildren();
        const crumbs = ["Region"];
        if (state.region) crumbs.push(regionNames[state.region]);
        if (currentGroup()) crumbs.push(currentGroup().name);
        if (state.lead) crumbs.push(state.lead.label);
        crumbs.forEach((label, index) => {
          if (index) nav.append(make("span", "crumb-sep", "›"));
          nav.append(make("span", `crumb${index === crumbs.length - 1 ? " current" : ""}`, label));
        });
      }

      function reset(fromTop = true) {
        state.region = null;
        state.groupIndex = null;
        state.lead = null;
        state.path = [];
        state.choices = [];
        renderRegions();
        updateHash();
        if (fromTop) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
      }

      function renderRegions() {
        showOnly("regionView");
        setHeading(1, "Choose a region", "All five Elite Four rerun routes are included.");
        updateBreadcrumbs();
        const wrap = make("div", "region-tabs");
        regionOrder.forEach(key => {
          const button = make("button", `tab${state.region === key ? " active" : ""}`, regionNames[key]);
          button.type = "button";
          button.addEventListener("click", () => selectRegion(key));
          wrap.append(button);
        });
        el("regionView").replaceChildren(wrap);
      }

      function selectRegion(key) {
        state.region = key;
        state.groupIndex = null;
        state.lead = null;
        state.path = [];
        state.choices = [];
        renderTrainers();
        updateHash();
      }

      function renderTrainers() {
        const data = currentData();
        showOnly("trainerView");
        setHeading(2, `Choose the ${regionNames[state.region]} opponent`, data.groupPrompt || "Choose the opponent:");
        updateBreadcrumbs();
        const grid = make("div", "trainer-grid");
        data.groups.forEach((group, index) => {
          const button = make("button", "trainer-card");
          button.type = "button";
          button.append(make("span", "trainer-number", String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")));
          button.append(make("span", "trainer-name", group.name));
          button.append(make("span", "trainer-meta", `${group.entries.length} possible leads`));
          button.addEventListener("click", () => selectTrainer(index));
          grid.append(button);
        });
        el("trainerView").replaceChildren(grid);
      }

      function selectTrainer(index) {
        state.groupIndex = index;
        state.lead = null;
        state.path = [];
        state.choices = [];
        renderLeads();
        updateHash();
      }

      function renderLeads(query = "") {
        const group = currentGroup();
        showOnly("leadView");
        setHeading(3, `What did ${group.name} lead with?`, "Search or choose the Pokémon shown at the start of battle.");
        updateBreadcrumbs();
        const container = document.createDocumentFragment();
        const searchWrap = make("div", "search-wrap");
        searchWrap.innerHTML = '<svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"></circle><path d="m20 20-3.5-3.5"></path></svg>';
        const input = make("input", "search");
        input.type = "search";
        input.placeholder = "Search Pokémon…";
        input.setAttribute("aria-label", "Search lead Pokémon");
        input.value = query;
        input.addEventListener("input", () => drawLeadButtons(input.value));
        searchWrap.append(input);
        container.append(searchWrap);
        const grid = make("div", "lead-grid");
        grid.id = "leadGrid";
        container.append(grid);
        el("leadView").replaceChildren(container);
        drawLeadButtons(query);
      }

      function drawLeadButtons(query) {
        const grid = el("leadGrid");
        const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
        const entries = currentGroup().entries.filter(item => item.label.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
        grid.replaceChildren();
        if (!entries.length) {
          const empty = make("p", "empty", "No lead Pokémon matches that search.");
          empty.style.gridColumn = "1 / -1";
          grid.append(empty);
          return;
        }
        entries.forEach(entry => {
          const button = make("button", "lead-button", entry.label);
          button.type = "button";
          button.addEventListener("click", () => selectLead(entry));
          grid.append(button);
        });
      }

      function selectLead(entry) {
        state.lead = entry;
        state.path = [entry.nodeId];
        state.choices = [];
        renderSolve();
        updateHash();
      }

      function chooseBranch(label, nodeId) {
        state.choices.push(label);
        state.path.push(nodeId);
        renderSolve();
        updateHash();
        requestAnimationFrame(() => document.querySelector(".decision, .complete")?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" }));
      }

      function goBack() {
        if (state.path.length <= 1) return;
        state.path.pop();
        state.choices.pop();
        renderSolve();
        updateHash();
      }

      function restartBattle() {
        state.path = [state.lead.nodeId];
        state.choices = [];
        renderSolve();
        updateHash();
      }

      function renderSolve() {
        const data = currentData();
        const group = currentGroup();
        showOnly("solveView");
        setHeading(4, `${group.name} led ${state.lead.label}`, "Complete the actions in order, then choose what the opponent did.");
        updateBreadcrumbs();
        const root = make("div", "walkthrough");

        state.path.forEach((nodeId, index) => {
          const node = data.nodes[nodeId];
          const card = make("article", `path-card${index < state.path.length - 1 ? " past" : ""}`);
          const head = make("div", "path-card-head");
          head.append(make("span", "turn-badge", String(index + 1)));
          head.append(make("strong", "", index === 0 ? "Opening actions" : "Next actions"));
          card.append(head);
          const list = make("ol", "actions");
          let actionNumber = 0;
          (node.steps || []).forEach(step => {
            const isSetup = step.kind === "setup";
            const item = make("li", `action${isSetup ? " setup" : ""}`);
            item.append(make("span", "action-num", isSetup ? "i" : String(++actionNumber)));
            item.append(make("span", "action-text", step.text));
            list.append(item);
          });
          if (!node.steps?.length) list.append(make("li", "empty", "No action text is listed for this node."));
          card.append(list);
          if (state.choices[index]) card.append(make("div", "response", `Opponent response: ${state.choices[index]}`));
          root.append(card);
        });

        const currentNode = data.nodes[state.path[state.path.length - 1]];
        if (currentNode.branch?.options?.length) {
          const decision = make("section", "decision");
          decision.append(make("h4", "", currentNode.branch.prompt || "What did the opponent do?"));
          const choices = make("div", "choices");
          currentNode.branch.options.forEach(option => {
            const button = make("button", "choice-button", option.label);
            button.type = "button";
            button.addEventListener("click", () => chooseBranch(option.label, option.nodeId));
            choices.append(button);
          });
          decision.append(choices);
          root.append(decision);
        } else {
          const complete = make("section", "complete");
          complete.append(make("strong", "", "Branch complete"));
          complete.append(make("p", "", "You reached the end of this source route. Keep following the final actions above."));
          root.append(complete);
        }

        const controls = make("div", "walk-controls");
        const back = make("button", "control", "← Previous branch");
        back.type = "button";
        back.disabled = state.path.length <= 1;
        back.addEventListener("click", goBack);
        const restart = make("button", "control", "Restart this battle");
        restart.type = "button";
        restart.addEventListener("click", restartBattle);
        const changeLead = make("button", "control primary", "Choose another lead");
        changeLead.type = "button";
        changeLead.addEventListener("click", () => { state.lead = null; state.path = []; state.choices = []; renderLeads(); updateHash(); });
        controls.append(back, restart, changeLead);
        root.append(controls);
        el("solveView").replaceChildren(root);
      }

      function restoreFromHash() {
        const raw = location.hash.replace(/^#/, "");
        if (!raw) return false;
        try {
          const [region, groupRaw, leadRaw, choicesRaw] = raw.split("/");
          if (!allData[region]) return false;
          state.region = region;
          const groupIndex = Number(groupRaw);
          if (!Number.isInteger(groupIndex) || !allData[region].groups[groupIndex]) { renderTrainers(); return true; }
          state.groupIndex = groupIndex;
          const leadLabel = leadRaw ? decodeURIComponent(leadRaw) : "";
          const lead = currentGroup().entries.find(item => item.label === leadLabel);
          if (!lead) { renderLeads(); return true; }
          state.lead = lead;
          state.path = [lead.nodeId];
          state.choices = [];
          const wantedChoices = choicesRaw ? choicesRaw.split("~").map(decodeURIComponent) : [];
          for (const label of wantedChoices) {
            const node = currentData().nodes[state.path[state.path.length - 1]];
            const option = node?.branch?.options?.find(item => item.label === label);
            if (!option) break;
            state.choices.push(label);
            state.path.push(option.nodeId);
          }
          renderSolve();
          return true;
        } catch { return false; }
      }

      const totals = Object.values(allData).reduce((acc, data) => {
        acc.leads += data.groups.reduce((sum, group) => sum + group.entries.length, 0);
        acc.nodes += Object.keys(data.nodes).length;
        return acc;
      }, { leads: 0, nodes: 0 });
      const warning = allData.sinnoh?.warning || Object.values(allData)[0]?.warning || "Use this guide for established rerun teams.";
      el("warningText").textContent = warning.replace(/^⚠️\s*/, "");
      el("stats").textContent = `${totals.leads.toLocaleString()} lead matchups • ${totals.nodes.toLocaleString()} decision nodes • 5 regions`;
      el("resetTop").addEventListener("click", () => reset(true));
      if (!restoreFromHash()) renderRegions();
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
